ac_release_AWW_start_network = {
	if = {
		limit = { ac_release_AWW_can_repair_network = yes }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -50

			set_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_AWW_network_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.200 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_cancel_network = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_finish_network = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_done } }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
	}
}

ac_release_REC_cancel_network = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_case0
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_case1
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_case2
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_network_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
	}
}

		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_network_active }

			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_SER_network_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.204 days = 90 }
		}
	}

					owns_state = 209 owns_state = 571
					all_owned_state = { OR = { state = 209 state = 571 } }
					controls_province = 516 controls_province = 618 controls_province = 816 controls_province = 992 controls_province = 1660 controls_province = 1759 controls_province = 2076 controls_province = 2323 controls_province = 2952 controls_province = 3484 controls_province = 3515 controls_province = 5878 controls_province = 9582
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_railway_connection = { path = { 1759 2076 } } } }
					build_railway = {
						level = 1
						path = { 1759 2076 }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_railway_connection = { path = { 1759 3484 } } } }
					build_railway = {
						level = 1
						path = { 1759 3484 }
					}
				}
				set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_network_done
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_network_case0
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_network_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_network_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
	}
}
ac_release_quote_maintenance_capacity = {
	set_temp_variable = { ac_release_free_mil = num_of_military_factories }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ac_release_free_mil = ac_release_reserved_mil }
	set_temp_variable = { ac_release_free_yards = num_of_naval_factories }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { ac_release_free_yards = ac_release_reserved_yards }
}
ac_release_AWW_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "AWW_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }

		}

		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = AWW_larry_foulke } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = AWW_larry_foulke } } }
			recruit_character = AWW_larry_foulke
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = AWW_joshua_bristow } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = AWW_joshua_bristow } } }
			recruit_character = AWW_joshua_bristow
		}

		ac_release_AWW_ensure_ruling_office = yes
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_physical_registered
	}
}

					fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = ship_fire_control_system_0
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = light_ship_engine_2
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_1
					rear_1_custom_slot = ship_depth_charge_1
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_licensed_escort_bba0_mtg1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_destroyer = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_battery = 1
				basic_depth_charges = 1
				basic_ship_hull_light = 1
				basic_torpedo = 1
				electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
				gw_artillery = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "AWW Release escort"
				type = ship_hull_light_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_battery_slot = ship_light_battery_1
					fixed_ship_anti_air_slot = ship_anti_air_1
					fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = ship_fire_control_system_0
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = light_ship_engine_2
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_1
					rear_1_custom_slot = ship_depth_charge_1
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}
}

ac_release_AWW_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.100 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target is_core_of = AWW is_fully_controlled_by = AWW free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target is_core_of = AWW is_fully_controlled_by = AWW } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_start_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }

			set_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_AWW_military_waiting days = 90 }

		}
	}

			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_target is_core_of = AWW is_fully_controlled_by = AWW } add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }

ac_release_REC_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "REC_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_REC_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = REC
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 12
			producer = REC
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = REC
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_transport_delivered
	}
}
ac_release_REC_physical_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_release_REC_prepare_maintenance = yes
		ac_release_REC_deliver_transport_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = REC_urs_gross } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = REC_urs_gross } } }
			recruit_character = REC_urs_gross
		}

		ac_release_REC_ensure_ruling_office = yes
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_physical_registered
	}
}

ac_release_REC_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.102 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_REC_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target is_core_of = REC is_fully_controlled_by = REC free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target is_core_of = REC is_fully_controlled_by = REC } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_REC_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active

		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_REC_start_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_military_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100

			set_country_flag = ac_release_REC_military_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_REC_military_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.103 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}

ac_release_REL_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "REL_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_REL_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = REL
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 12
			producer = REL
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = REL
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_transport_delivered
	}
}
ac_release_REL_physical_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_release_REL_prepare_maintenance = yes
		ac_release_REL_deliver_transport_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = REL_ac_government_office } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = REL_ac_government_office } } }
			recruit_character = REL_ac_government_office
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic has_character = REL_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = REL_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = REL_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism has_character = REL_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = REL_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = socialism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = REL_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism has_character = REL_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = REL_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = fascism_ideology
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = REL_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality has_character = REL_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = REL_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism_na
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = REL_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_physical_registered
	}
}

ac_release_REL_license_trucks = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL ac_release_maintenance_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba0_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				motorised_infantry = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba0_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba0_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				motorised_infantry = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba0_mtg1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba1_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				motorised_infantry = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba1_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				motorised_infantry = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_trucks_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}
}
ac_release_REL_certify_trucks = {
	if = {
		limit = { ac_release_REL_can_certify_trucks = yes }
		ac_release_REL_license_trucks = yes
		add_political_power = -5
	}
}
ac_release_REL_queue_trucks = {
	if = {
		limit = { ac_release_REL_can_queue_trucks = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = rubber amount >= 1 } has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 1 }
			}
			ac_release_REL_license_trucks = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = motorized_equipment_1
					creator = REL
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_queue_trucks
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns"
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = rubber amount >= 1 } has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 1 }
			}
			ac_release_REL_license_trucks = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = motorized_equipment_1
					creator = REL
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_queue_trucks
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = rubber amount >= 1 } has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 1 }
			}
			ac_release_REL_license_trucks = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = motorized_equipment_1
					creator = REL
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_queue_trucks
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns"
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = rubber amount >= 1 } has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 1 }
			}
			ac_release_REL_license_trucks = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = motorized_equipment_1
					creator = REL
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_queue_trucks
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
	}
}

ac_release_REL_license_fighter = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL ac_release_maintenance_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba0_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				fighter1 = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba0_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba0_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				fighter1 = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba0_mtg1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba1_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				aa_lmg = 1
				basic_small_airframe = 1
				engines_2 = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "REL Release fighter"
				type = small_plane_airframe_1
				modules = {
					fixed_main_weapon_slot = light_mg_4x
					engine_type_slot = engine_2_1x
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba1_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				aa_lmg = 1
				basic_small_airframe = 1
				engines_2 = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "REL Release fighter"
				type = small_plane_airframe_1
				modules = {
					fixed_main_weapon_slot = light_mg_4x
					engine_type_slot = engine_2_1x
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_licensed_fighter_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}
}

ac_release_REL_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.104 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_REL_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL has_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target is_core_of = REL is_fully_controlled_by = REL free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target is_core_of = REL is_fully_controlled_by = REL } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_REL_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_REL_civilian_target }
	}
}

ac_release_SBK_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "SBK_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_SBK_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = SBK
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 12
			producer = SBK
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = SBK
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_transport_delivered
	}
}
ac_release_SBK_physical_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_release_SBK_prepare_maintenance = yes
		ac_release_SBK_deliver_transport_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = SBK_ac_government_office } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = SBK_ac_government_office } } }
			recruit_character = SBK_ac_government_office
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism has_character = SBK_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SBK_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = socialism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SBK_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism has_character = SBK_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SBK_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = fascism_ideology
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SBK_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality has_character = SBK_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SBK_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism_na
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SBK_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = monarchism has_character = SBK_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SBK_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = constitutional_monarchy
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SBK_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_physical_registered
	}
}

				basic_ship_hull_submarine = 1
				early_ship_hull_submarine = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "SBK Release submarine"
				type = ship_hull_submarine_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_sub_1
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = sub_ship_engine_2
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_licensed_submarine_bba0_mtg1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_licensed_submarine_bba1_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_submarine = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_licensed_submarine_bba1_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_licensed_submarine_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_ship_hull_submarine = 1
				early_ship_hull_submarine = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "SBK Release submarine"
				type = ship_hull_submarine_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_sub_1
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = sub_ship_engine_2
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_licensed_submarine_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}
}

ac_release_SBK_queue_submarine = {
	if = {
		limit = { ac_release_SBK_can_queue_submarine = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 2 }
			}
			ac_release_SBK_license_submarine = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = submarine_2
					creator = SBK
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_queue_submarine
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns"
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 2 }
			}
			ac_release_SBK_license_submarine = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = ship_hull_submarine_2
					creator = SBK
					version_name = "SBK Release submarine"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_queue_submarine
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 2 }
			}
			ac_release_SBK_license_submarine = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = submarine_2
					creator = SBK
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_queue_submarine
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns"
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount >= 2 }
			}
			ac_release_SBK_license_submarine = yes
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = ship_hull_submarine_2
					creator = SBK
					version_name = "SBK Release submarine"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_queue_submarine
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
	}

ac_release_SBK_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.106 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_SBK_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target is_core_of = SBK is_fully_controlled_by = SBK free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target is_core_of = SBK is_fully_controlled_by = SBK } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_SBK_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_civilian_target }
	}
}

			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SBK_military_target is_core_of = SBK is_fully_controlled_by = SBK } add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes } }

ac_release_SER_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "SER_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_SER_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = SER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 12
			producer = SER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = SER
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_transport_delivered
	}
}
ac_release_SER_physical_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_release_SER_prepare_maintenance = yes
		ac_release_SER_deliver_transport_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = SER_ac_government_office } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = SER_ac_government_office } } }
			recruit_character = SER_ac_government_office
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic has_character = SER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism has_character = SER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = socialism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism has_character = SER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = fascism_ideology
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality has_character = SER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = SER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism_na
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = SER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_physical_registered
	}
}

ac_release_SER_license_escort = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER ac_release_maintenance_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba0_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_destroyer = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba0_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba0_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_battery = 1
				basic_depth_charges = 1
				basic_ship_hull_light = 1
				basic_torpedo = 1
				electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
				gw_artillery = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "SER Release escort"
				type = ship_hull_light_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_battery_slot = ship_light_battery_1
					fixed_ship_anti_air_slot = ship_anti_air_1
					fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = ship_fire_control_system_0
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = light_ship_engine_2
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_1
					rear_1_custom_slot = ship_depth_charge_1
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba0_mtg1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				NOT = { has_dlc = "Man the Guns" } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg0 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_destroyer = 1
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_battery = 1
				basic_depth_charges = 1
				basic_ship_hull_light = 1
				basic_torpedo = 1
				electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
				gw_artillery = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "SER Release escort"
				type = ship_hull_light_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_battery_slot = ship_light_battery_1
					fixed_ship_anti_air_slot = ship_anti_air_1
					fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = ship_fire_control_system_0
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = light_ship_engine_2
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_1
					rear_1_custom_slot = ship_depth_charge_1
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}

ac_release_SER_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.108 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_SER_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target is_core_of = SER is_fully_controlled_by = SER free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target is_core_of = SER is_fully_controlled_by = SER } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_SER_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SER_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_SER_start_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_military_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_SER_military_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.109 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_SER_finish_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_military_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target is_core_of = SER is_fully_controlled_by = SER free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target is_core_of = SER is_fully_controlled_by = SER } add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_military_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target }
	}
}
ac_release_SER_cancel_military = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SER_military_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SER_military_target }

ac_release_SHI_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "SHI_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_SHI_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = SHI
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {

			amount = 12
			producer = SHI
		}

			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = SHI
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_transport_delivered
	}
}

				has_dlc = "Man the Guns" NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1 }
			}
			set_technology = {
				basic_battery = 1
				basic_depth_charges = 1
				basic_ship_hull_light = 1
				basic_torpedo = 1
				electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
				gw_artillery = 1
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "SHI Release escort"
				type = ship_hull_light_2
				modules = {
					fixed_ship_battery_slot = ship_light_battery_1
					fixed_ship_anti_air_slot = ship_anti_air_1
					fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = ship_fire_control_system_0
					fixed_ship_engine_slot = light_ship_engine_2
					fixed_ship_torpedo_slot = ship_torpedo_1
					rear_1_custom_slot = ship_depth_charge_1
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_licensed_escort_bba1_mtg1
		}
	}
}

ac_release_SHI_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.110 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_SHI_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI has_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target is_core_of = SHI is_fully_controlled_by = SHI free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target is_core_of = SHI is_fully_controlled_by = SHI } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_SHI_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_civilian_target }
	}
}

			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_SHI_military_target is_core_of = SHI is_fully_controlled_by = SHI } add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_military_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_SHI_military_active

ac_release_TER_prepare_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_maintenance_registered } }
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_engineers = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_train = 1
			motorised_infantry = 1
			gw_artillery = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1
			fuel_silos = 1
			fuel_refining = 1
		}
		load_oob = "TER_release_templates"
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_mil = 0 }
		set_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 0 }
		set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_maintenance_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_maintenance_registered }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_maintenance_registered
	}
}
ac_release_TER_deliver_transport_reserve = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_was_absent ac_release_owned_capital = yes any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_transport_delivered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_annexed } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 2
			producer = TER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment_1
			amount = 12
			producer = TER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = convoy_1
			amount = 6
			producer = TER
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_transport_delivered
	}
}
ac_release_TER_physical_registration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER has_capitulated = no has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_was_absent NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_adopted } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_annexed } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_physical_registered } any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_release_TER_prepare_maintenance = yes
		ac_release_TER_deliver_transport_reserve = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = TER_ac_government_office } NOT = { any_country = { has_character = TER_ac_government_office } } }
			recruit_character = TER_ac_government_office
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic has_character = TER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = TER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = TER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism has_character = TER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = TER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = socialism
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = TER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism has_character = TER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = TER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = fascism_ideology
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = TER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality has_character = TER_ac_government_office }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = TER_ac_government_office
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = centrism_na
					expire = "2066.1.1.1"
					traits = { }
					desc = TER_ac_government_office_desc
				}
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_physical_registered
	}
}

					creator = TER
					version_name = "TER Release submarine"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0
				efficiency = 50
			}

			set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_queue_submarine
			add_to_variable = { ac_release_reserved_yards = 1 }
			add_political_power = -15
		}
	}
}
ac_release_TER_start_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER ac_release_can_invest = yes NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_built } }
		ac_econ_try_fund_release_project = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_econ_release_project_funded }
			add_political_power = -100
			capital_scope = { set_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target }
			set_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active
			set_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
			set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_waiting days = 90 }
			country_event = { id = ac_release.112 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}
ac_release_TER_finish_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_waiting } }
		if = {
			limit = { tag = TER original_tag = TER has_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_waiting } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_built } has_capitulated = no any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target is_core_of = TER is_fully_controlled_by = TER free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 } } }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target is_core_of = TER is_fully_controlled_by = TER } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_built
		}
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target }
	}
}
ac_release_TER_cancel_civilian = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active }
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_active
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_waiting
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_project_active
		set_country_flag = { flag = ac_release_project_cooldown days = 30 }
		every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target } clr_state_flag = ac_release_TER_civilian_target }
	}
}
